的证据可以近似独立累加。但如果被 mask 的 token 之间条件依赖强，例如短语内部、实体内部、局部语法强相关位置，token-wise 概率乘积会偏离目标 joint probability。作者用累计条件互信息刻画这种结构性误差，并指出该误差是非负的，可能掩盖训练样本与非训练样本之间较弱的记忆差异。直接估计所有 token 子集的 CMI 代价很高，所以 ITS 用模型内部 attention 构造 pairwise dependency proxy，优先选择内部依赖弱的 token 子集。为了避免每轮都选到相似位置，方法还加入 diversity-promoting penalty，让多轮采样覆盖互补 token 区域。相比随机 mask，ITS 的核心差异是把 mask set 当成检测信号的一部分来优化，而不是把它看作无结构的查询噪声。","experiments":"实验覆盖 diffusion LLM 的训练数据检测场景，采用灰盒设定：审计方可以向目标模型提交任意部分 mask 的序列，并观察 masked token 的预测分布；同时有预训练参考模型和微调后目标模型。论文报告 ITS 在多数据集上稳定强于随机采样等基线，并在 LoRA 这类稀疏模型更新、以及只能观测部分输入的更困难条件下保持鲁棒。给出的全文片段没有包含完整表格和具体 AUC / TPR 数字，因此不宜补写精确幅度。较有价值的实验点是：性能提升来自“低依赖 token 子集”而不只是更多采样；diversity penalty 改善有限预算下的 token 覆盖；在微调痕迹较弱的 LoRA 场景中，依赖控制对检测信号更关键。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。重点看三处：第一，概率近似误差与累计 CMI 的推导，判断理论假设是否适合实际 dLLM；第二，ITS 如何从 attention 得到 pairwise dependency proxy，以及 diversity penalty 的形式；第三，LoRA 和 partial observation 设置下的消融实验。可以略读常规隐私和版权背景。值得深挖的问题是 attention dependency proxy 是否足够稳定，以及该采样策略能否迁移到 masked / any-order LM 的困惑度估计和数据筛选。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies training-data detection for diffusion LMs under a limited query budget. Unlike autoregressive LMs, diffusion LMs do not provide a cheap left-to-right likelihood decomposition. Detection therefore depends on which tokens are masked and queried. The key issue is that randomly masked tokens are not independent, and aggregating their token-wise reconstruction probabilities introduces a structural estimation error.","importance":"The main value is methodological. It clarifies a privacy-auditing problem that is specific to diffusion and masked language models: likelihood-like signals are configuration-dependent. The paper frames random masking as an approximation to a joint probability and shows that the approximation error is governed by cumulative conditional mutual information among the sampled masked tokens. This is useful for researchers working on diffusion LMs, masked LMs, data leakage auditing, and copyright-oriented membership detection.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is Independent Token Sampling, or ITS. The analysis starts from the observation that random token sampling implicitly treats the masked token set as if its token-level probability contributions could be accumulated independently. When the sampled positions are strongly dependent, such as tokens inside a phrase, entity, or local syntactic unit, this surrogate probability becomes biased and can blur weak memorization signals. Directly searching for low-CMI token subsets is computationally expensive, so ITS uses the model’s attention patterns as a pairwise dependency proxy. It then selects masked token subsets with low estimated internal dependency. A diversity-promoting penalty is added to avoid repeatedly selecting similar positions across sampling rounds, improving coverage under a fixed query budget. The distinction from prior random masking methods is that the masked subset is optimized as part of the detector rather than treated as sampling noise.","experiments":"The experiments follow a grey-box training-data detection setup: an auditor can query the target diffusion LM with arbitrary partially masked sequences and observe masked-token predictions, with access to a pretrained reference model and a fine-tuned target model. The paper reports consistent gains over random-sampling baselines across multiple datasets. It also evaluates harder settings such as sparse LoRA updates and partial observations, where memorization signals are weaker. The supplied full-text excerpt does not include the detailed result tables or exact AUC / TPR numbers, so the safe conclusion is qualitative: controlling token dependency improves detection reliability, and the diversity term helps use the sampling budget more effectively.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Focus on the derivation connecting structural estimation error to cumulative conditional mutual information, the construction of the attention-based dependency proxy, and the ablations under LoRA and partial-observation settings. The broader privacy and copyright motivation can be read quickly. A useful follow-up question is whether attention is a sufficiently stable proxy for dependency, and whether the same token-subset selection idea can improve likelihood estimation or data filtering for masked and any-order LMs.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看的是误差分解，不是隐私口号：它说明 dLLM 的检测难点来自 mask token 依赖而非 query 不够，多看理论段和误差实验，能直接指导你做 diffusion LM 审计。","en":"Read it for the error decomposition, not the privacy framing: it shows the bottleneck is masked-token dependence rather than just query budget, which is directly useful for auditing diffusion LMs."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongyao Yu","Tianqu Zhuang","Ziyuan Xu","Hao Fang","Jiaxin Hong","Bin Chen","Shu-Tao Xia"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","membership-inference","memorization","conditional-mutual-information","data-audit"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22145","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.22145","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.22161","title":"Didactic knowledge or Clinical Cases? Clinical data comes from vignette-style MedMCQA items and MIMIC-III records processed through a Temporal Care Simulation. In this simulation, information is masked by care stage, such as admission, hospitalization, and discharge, so the model only sees what would be available at that time. 的核心问题，是构造一个可控的双语预训练框架和成体系模型族，让研究者能沿训练轨迹观察跨语言迁移何时出现、暴露顺序如何影响 L2 行为，以及双语收益到底来自真实 transfer 还是仅仅来自数据配比差异。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要有两层。第一层是方法学价值。它把 exposure schedule 明确提升为和数据规模、tokenizer、架构并列的一等实验变量，这对 size、tokenizer 和 overall language mixture，只改变两个核心因素：L1 是什么，以及两种语言在预训练中以什么顺序出现。英语固定为 L2，L1 覆盖 21 种语言。训练条件包含 5 种 exposure regime，覆盖 joint/interleaved 与更有阶段性的 ordered curricula；同时在 100M、2B、24B token 三个 FineWeb 规模上训练，并额外提供 human-scale 的 100M 条件。另一个重要设计是 matched monolingual baselines：作者为 20 种语言提供单语对照，使得“双语优于单语”不再被总 token 数或语料构成混淆。框架层面，它统一了此前文献中的 structured exposure 和 sequential bilingual training law 之外的独立轴；二，早期 checkpoint 是否能揭示 transfer emergence 的相变点；三，matched monolingual control 这种设计能否迁移到更一般的 multilingual / continual pretrain 研究。泛读大约 25–35 分钟，精读实验图表和附录会更值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not trying to build yet another bilingual LM. Its real target is experimental control. Prior bilingual and L2-LM studies usually change exposure schedule, language pair, scale, tokenizer, and continual-learning setup at the same time, so observed effects are hard to attribute. Beetle addresses this by building a controlled bilingual pretraining framework where tokenizer, target language, training budget, and exposure structure can be manipulated separately. The central question is when cross-lingual transfer emerges during training, and how much of bilingual behavior is caused by exposure timing rather than by data amount or model configuration.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mainly methodological. It elevates exposure schedule into a first-class variable, alongside scale and data mixture, which matters for multilingual and continual pretraining. Many differences that look like language effects may actually be timeline effects: when a language is introduced, how gradually it is mixed in, and how long it remains dominant. The second contribution is the resource itself: 285 bilingual models, 45 monolingual controls, and full checkpoint trajectories. Continued pretraining (CPT) is the natural remedy, but it is typically evaluated through a continual learning lens that assumes disjoint data streams. This is a poor fit for time-incremental updates on web-scale crawls, where successive snapshots share substantial URL overlap by design. We study time-incremental CPT in this realistic regime: continued pretrain","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-20T22:44:54Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_23916.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很实际的 continued pretraining 问题：模型知识截止后，能否用后续 web crawl 做时间增量更新，在吸收新知识的同时不损害已有知识和通用能力。作者指出，很多 continual learning 设置假设数据流彼此不重叠，但真实 web-scale 更新并不是这样。Common Crawl 等管线天然存在跨时间快照的 URL 重叠，且开源模型通常没有完整预训练语料可查，因此只能用时间戳近似定义“新数据”。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练研究的价值在于把 CPT 从抽象的灾难性遗忘问题，拉回到可操作的 web-scale 更新场景。主要结论偏乐观：在严格使用 cutoff 之后数据的设定下，模型可以获得新知识；已有知识没有明显遗忘；13 个通用 benchmark 的平均表现基本保持稳定。更有用的是 recipe 层面的发现：数据质量比数据量更关键；知识吸收和通用能力保持对应的学习率最优点相差约一个数量级；足够 rank 的 LoRA 可以接近 full CPT，同时节省显存；训练收益在前 1/3 左右趋于平台。这些结论直接影响持续预训练的数据预算、学习率选择、参数更新方式和更新频率。","technical_approach":"作者定义 time-incremental CPT：只使用每个模型知识截止日期之后的 FineWeb-Edu dump 继续训练。实验覆盖六个开源模型，来自三个家族和四个规模：OLMo2 1B/7B、Llama-3.2 1B/3B、Llama-3.1 8B、Gemma-3 1B。这个选择有两个好处。第一，OLMo2 提供更高的数据透明度，可以帮助校验数据时间和分布问题。第二，Llama 与 Gemma 提供不同训练配方和数据闭源程度下的外推测试。评测分两类：一类是从每日新闻构造的问题，用于同时测试 cutoff 前知识保持和 cutoff 后知识获取；另一类是 13 个标准 LLM benchmark，用于观察通用能力是否被 CPT 损伤。方法上，作者比较不同数据规模和质量、不同学习率、full CPT 与 LoRA、以及 CPT 后再接 SFT/DPO 的部署链路。关键区别是它没有刻意构造互斥数据流，而是保留 realistic crawl 中不可避免的时间快照重叠。这个设定更接近实际模型维护，也更能解释为什么传统 continual learning 中的强遗忘结论未必适用于 LLM 的短周期 web CPT。","experiments":"实验结果围绕四个问题展开。第一，模型确实吸收新知识。OLMo2 和 Gemma-3 1B 的提升最明显，Llama 系列提升较有限。cutoff 前问题上，六个模型里有五个也有提升，说明新数据没有系统性覆盖旧知识；更近期事件上的优势更大，并且在源文章不在训练语料中的问题上仍能保留收益，说明效果不只是简单记忆训练样本。第二，通用能力代价很小。13 个下游任务的 macro-average accuracy 对所有模型都保持在 base 模型 0.01 以内，13 个任务中有 11 个维持在 ±0.02 范围内。第三，训练 recipe 有明显取舍。高质量 6B token 子集已经捕获大部分可用信号，继续扩大数量的边际收益低于提升质量；知识获取和通用能力保持无法由同一个学习率同时最优，两个最优区间约相差一个数量级；足够 rank 的 LoRA 能匹配 full CPT，并显著降低 GPU memory；训练早期收益集中，约前 1/3 后开始平台化，支持短周期、重复更新的运行方式。第四，CPT 收益可以穿过后续 SFT。DPO 的影响依模型家族而变：在 Llama-3.1 8B 上会部分侵蚀 CPT 带来的知识收益，在 OLMo2 7B 上反而进一步改善。这提示 post-training 可能改写 CPT 学到的新知识，不能只在 pre-SFT checkpoint 上评估。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction 中对 time-incremental CPT 的问题定义，尤其是为什么 disjoint continual learning 不适合真实 web 更新。Methodology 需要关注三点：每个模型 cutoff 如何确定，FineWeb-Edu post-cutoff 数据如何切分，新闻问答评测如何避免直接训练集命中。实验部分优先读 Sec 3.1 的新旧知识结果、Sec 3.3 的 13 benchmark 能力保持、Sec 3.4 的数据质量/学习率/LoRA 消融、Sec 3.5 的 SFT 与 DPO 链路。最值得深挖的是学习率冲突和训练早期平台化：前者决定是否需要双阶段 schedule 或任务混合退火，后者决定持续预训练是否应采用短而频繁的更新，而不是一次长跑。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical form of continued pretraining: after a model’s knowledge cutoff, can post-cutoff web data update its knowledge without erasing old knowledge or degrading general capabilities? The key framing is that realistic web updates are time-incremental, not disjoint continual-learning streams. Web snapshots share URLs and distributional structure, and for most open-weight models the original pretraining corpus is unavailable, so timestamp-based updating is the operational constraint.","importance":"The main value is a realistic CPT study at the web-crawl level. The findings are actionable for base-model maintenance. Post-cutoff CPT can add new knowledge with little evidence of catastrophic forgetting. General benchmark performance remains nearly unchanged. The recipe-level results matter more than the headline gains: data quality dominates raw token count; the learning-rate optimum for knowledge acquisition and the optimum for preserving general capability are separated by roughly an order of magnitude; sufficiently high-rank LoRA can match full CPT while reducing memory; most gains appear early, suggesting short repeated update runs rather than long monolithic CPT jobs.","technical_approach":"The authors define time-incremental CPT as continued pretraining only on FineWeb-Edu dumps dated after each model’s knowledge cutoff. They evaluate six open-weight models across three families and several scales: OLMo2 1B/7B, Llama-3.2 1B/3B, Llama-3.1 8B, and Gemma-3 1B. The evaluation has two parts. News-derived questions probe both pre-cutoff retention and post-cutoff acquisition. A suite of 13 standard LLM benchmarks measures whether general capabilities are preserved. The study then varies data size and data quality, learning rate, full-parameter CPT versus LoRA, and the downstream deployment chain with SFT and DPO. The important methodological choice is not to force artificial disjoint data streams. The setup preserves the overlap and distributional continuity of real crawls, which is exactly where many deployed-model update pipelines operate.","experiments":"$d","reading_guide":"Read closely. Despite their effectiveness, we find that LoopLMs remain prone to loop instability: unstable refinement across iterations can produce localized uncertain \"hard\" tokens associated with reasoning errors. reasonin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-21T07:10:28Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_24196.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注 Looped Language Model 的一个很具体但很关键的问题：模型通过共享权重的多轮 latent refinement 做“隐式推理”时，并不是所有 token 都会随着 loop 次数增加而稳定变好。作者发现，少量 reasoning-critical token 会出现 loop instability：有的 token 过早自信，后续修正失败；有的 token 在不同迭代间来回摆动；这些局部不稳定会沿着推理轨迹传播，最后拖垮答案。论文要解决的就是，能否不改训练、不加外部模型，只利用 LoopLM 自己在不同 loop 里的内部动态，在推理时把这些 hard token 处理得更稳。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有两层。第一，它说明 latent reasoning/looped computation 不是“多算几步自然更好”这么简单，推理深度带来的收益和不稳定性是同时存在的，后者集中在少数关键 token 上。第二，它把 contrastive decoding 从“强模型减弱模型”的外部对比，改成“同一个模型不同递归阶段”的内部对比，这对未来做 recurrent Transformer、adaptive depth、test-time compute 分配、latent CoT 都有参考价值。虽然这篇更偏 inference method，不是 pretrain recipe 论文，但它揭示了 looped architecture 的训练后行为：早期迭代保留了有用的中间信号，晚期迭代并不总是单调支配早期迭代。这一点对设计 looped LM 的训练目标和停止准则都有意义。","technical_approach":"方法很直接。作者先分析 LoopLM 的 token-level refinement dynamics。模型结构是典型的 looped Transformer：prelude 和 coda 是非共享层，中间 k 层 block 在深度方向递归复用 d 次，因此参数深度是 p+k+l，但实现计算深度是 p+kd+l。论文主要在 Huginn-0125 这类 (2, 4⊗32, 2) 配置上做实验。\n\n作者先定义 trace entropy，用来衡量一个 token 在不同 loop 迭代上的不确定性轨迹。实验显示，错误推理轨迹通常有更高的 trace entropy。然后借助 logit lens 观察不同迭代的 top-1 / top-2 margin 变化，作者把 token 分成 easy 和 hard 两类：easy token 的预测会随迭代单调变稳；hard token 则常出现“先过早自信、后尝试修正”或“后期 refinement 失稳”的模式。论文指出 hard token 大约只占 reasoning trace 的 10%–15%，但对最终正确率影响很大。\n\nLoopCD 的核心做法，是把标准 contrastive decoding 的“expert vs amateur”替换成“同一 LoopLM 的最终迭代 vs 较早迭代”。直觉上，最终迭代通常更 refined，但较早迭代保留了尚未被后期错误 refinement 放大的中间信号。于是作者用最后一次 loop 的 logits 作为主分布，再用较早 loop 的 logits 构造对比项，形成最终采样分布。这样做不需要额外 amateur model，也不需要重新前向，因为这些中间 hidden states / logits 本来就在 loop 过程中已经算出来了，所以额外开销很小。\n\n从全文描述看，LoopCD 不是对所有 token 一视同仁地强行改分布，而是利用 loop-wise discrepancy 去更有针对性地修正 hard token。作者还做了 selective-intervention analysis，说明收益主要集中在这些高不确定、对推理更敏感的位置。这个设计比外部 CD 更贴合 LoopLM，因为它利用的是同一模型内部不同“思考阶段”的分歧，而不是两个不同能力模型之间的分歧。","experiments":"论文在 arithmetic reasoning、code generation、question answering 等多类任务上评估 LoopCD，并报告它持续优于标准解码和若干更强的 decoding baseline。给出的全文片段里没有完整主表数字，但有几组关键现象值得记住。\n\n第一，trace entropy 与正确性显著相关：在 GSM8K 上，错误推理轨迹的 entropy 分布整体更高，说明 LoopLM 的内部不稳定性确实能从 token-level uncertainty 读出来。\n\n第二，hard token 只占大约 10%–15% 的 reasoning trace，却承担了 disproportionate 的错误传播作用。作者在 Table 1 做了随机 token replacement：替换 hard token 带来的准确率下降明显大于替换 easy token，说明输出对 hard token 扰动更敏感。这是全文最关键的因果性证据之一，因为它说明 hard token 不是简单的相关现象，而是更接近性能瓶颈。\n\n第三，LoopCD 的收益主要来自 hard token。作者在 Section 5.4 做 selective intervention，显示如果只在这些 token 上施加 LoopCD，已经能拿到主要增益；对 easy token 干预则收益有限。这支持了方法设计的针对性。\n\n第四，方法几乎没有额外推理成本。因为 LoopLM 在 loop 过程中本来就会产生各轮 hidden states 和 logits，LoopCD 只是重用这些中间结果做对比，不像传统 CD 那样需要再跑一个 amateur model。对 test-time compute 敏感的 latent reasoning 场景，这是一个实际优点。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点看作者如何把“loop instability”从直觉现象变成可操作的 decoding 机会。建议先看 Introduction 和 Motivation，尤其是 Fig. 2：其中 (a) 是 trace entropy 与正确/错误轨迹的关系，(b) 是 easy/hard token 的 refinement pattern，(c) 是 hard token 与高熵的对应关系。这三张图基本构成了整篇论文的因果链。\n\n然后看 Method 部分，弄清楚 LoopCD 到底对比的是哪些 loop 的 logits、如何形成最终分布，以及为什么它不需要额外模型。若只想抓住方法，不必陷入所有公式细节。实验部分优先看 Table 1 和 selective-intervention analysis，它们最能说明 hard token 假设是否站得住。若你关心 latent reasoning 的训练设计，可以顺手思考一个延伸问题：既然早期 loop 保留了有用信号，训练时是否应该显式约束不同 loop 的 refinement 单调性，或者学习 token-wise adaptive stopping。\n\n预计阅读时间 20–30 分钟。读完后最值得带走的不是“又一个 decoding trick”，而是 LoopLM 内部不同迭代之间存在可利用的结构化分歧。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode of Looped Language Models: additional latent refinement steps do not always improve token predictions monotonically. A small subset of reasoning-critical tokens exhibits loop instability, including premature confidence, failed revision, and unstable late-stage refinement. These local errors can propagate through the reasoning trace and damage the final answer. The paper asks whether this can be mitigated at inference time, without retraining and without an external auxiliary model, by exploiting the model’s own intermediate loop dynamics.","importance":"The main relevance is conceptual. It shows that latent reasoning with shared-weight recurrence is not simply “more loops, better reasoning.” Extra test-time compute introduces both refinement and instability, and the instability is concentrated on a small set of hard tokens. The second contribution is methodological: contrastive decoding is internalized. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressiv","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T05:30:46Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22098.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TreeSpark 解决的是半自回归 speculative decoding 中“树形草稿如何既有效又不破坏采样正确性”的问题。已有 draft tree 往往按每个位置的边缘概率扩展节点，忽略候选 token 具体接在哪个父节点后面；对半自回归 drafter 来说，这会产生大量排序错误的分支。另一个问题是固定树大小不适合真实 serving：每轮可承受的验证成本、当前 batch/load、候选接受率都会变化。","importance":"这是一篇推理加速论文，但对基座研究仍有参考价值。它展示了半自回归 drafter 的一个实用接口：主干一次前向给出各深度 base logits，再用轻量 Markov head 做父节点条件化。这个设计把“并行出块”和“前缀条件依赖”拆开，避免为了构造树反复跑 drafter backbone。更重要的是，论文把 speculative decoding 的收益拆成三个可控变量：条件概率质量、接受率校准、验证预算。对训练小 drafter、设计 semi-AR auxiliary head、评估 serving 端真实收益都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 DSpark drafter 上。DSpark 用小型双向 backbone 对一个 block 的 mask 位置做一次前向，得到每个 draft 深度的 hidden state 和 base logits U_d；再用低秩 Markov head B(x,v)=<W1[x],W2[v]> 给“父 token x 到候选 token v”的转移加 bias。TreeSpark 的关键是：构造树时不直接用每个深度的边缘分布，而是对每个具体父节点调用已有 Markov head，得到 parent-conditioned child distribution。这样只需要 embedding lookup 和 rank-r matrix-vector product，不需要额外 backbone 前向，也不需要重新训练。节点价值不是直接用 raw q0，而是用 target verification 产生的边标签做两参数校准：p_hat = sigmoid(a logit(q0)+b)。校准只在祖先已被接受的边上拟合，因为这才是路径存活时真正会遇到的分布。树扩展按路径上 p_hat 的乘积排序，当最佳剩余路径存活概率低于阈值 theta 时停止。theta 由 serving runtime 根据负载选择。对非零温采样，TreeSpark 不用 deterministic top-k children，因为那不是合法 proposal sample；它从 residual q_T 中无放回抽取 siblings，并在验证时按共享 logits 和抽样顺序重建 proposal，再做 recursive rejection sampling，从而保持目标模型分布不变。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3-4B、8B、14B，以及六个主要 benchmark，包含 greedy 和 T∈{0,0.5,1} 的采样设置。关键结果有四点。第一，parent conditioning 很重要：在相同验证预算下，相比 DDTree，TreeSpark 的 accepted tokens per target forward 提升 9%–16%；如果只用边缘分布构树，哪怕给到 DSpark chain 8 倍验证预算，仍可能不如原 chain。第二，校准有效：Qwen3-4B 的两参数校准在 held-out edges 上达到 ECE 0.0105。第三，自适应停止优于固定预算：在不同温度下，adaptive stopping 位于匹配 fixed-budget acceptance-verification-cost frontier 之上。第四，端到端收益受负载影响明显：Qwen3-4B 单请求 wall-clock 中，最终实现相比 DSpark chain 接受长度提升 15%–25%，速度提升 8%–14%；但 serving 测量显示，大固定树在 batch size 增大后可能变成负收益，controller 会缩小树，最后退回 chain。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 分钟左右。重点看方法部分的 Markov-conditioned tree construction，理解它如何在一次 semi-AR backbone 前向后为不同父节点恢复条件分布。然后看 3.3 的非零温采样正确性，这是很多 speculative tree 工作容易忽略的点。3.4 和系统控制部分值得看，因为它把 acceptance calibration 和 runtime load 连接起来。实验部分优先看 conditioning、calibration、fixed budget 的消融，以及 single-request 和 batched serving 的对比。若只关心预训练，可重点吸收 drafter head 设计和校准信号构造，不必深挖工程实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TreeSpark addresses a practical weakness in semi-autoregressive speculative decoding: how to build a draft tree that is both useful and distribution-preserving. Existing draft trees often expand nodes using per-depth marginal probabilities, which ignore the parent token that a candidate extends. For semi-autoregressive drafters, this produces poorly ranked branches. A second issue is budget control: a fixed tree size does not match the varying acceptance rate, decoding round, batch size, or serving load.","importance":"The paper is mainly about inference acceleration, but it is relevant to base-model researchers who care about auxiliary drafters and semi-autoregressive heads. It gives a clean interface between a one-pass block drafter and parent-conditioned token prediction. The broader lesson is that speculative speedups depend on three separable components: conditional proposal quality, calibrated acceptance estimates, and runtime verification budget. This is useful when designing small drafters, semi-AR training objectives, or realistic serving evaluations.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"The experiments use Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B across six main benchmarks, with greedy decoding and sampled decoding at T∈{0,0.5,1}. History summarization compresses stale conversation turns when the context window fills. 分别来自哪里，最终如何组合成闭式分数。这里决定了方法是不是一个真正新的路由框架，还是把已有经验规则重新包装。\n\n第三看效率实验和消融。尤其关注 128K 下 routing 时间、总 prefill latency、与 FlashAttention 和其他 sparse baselines 的端到端比较。如果只看 FLOPs 或理论复杂度，信息是不够的。\n\n这篇适合 30–45 how to make block-sparse attention both accurate and actually faster than dense attention without retraining the model. The bottleneck is not the sparse kernel itself, but block routing. Existing training-free routers usually pool token representations after RoPE and use the pooled vectors to estimate block relevance. The authors argue that this is the wrong place to aggregate. RoPE injects position through phase rotations, and high-frequency dimensions vary rapidly even across nearby tokens. Pooling in post-RoPE space causes phase cancellation, which weakens local positional cues and distorts block affinity estimates. Prior fixes rely on token-level search or post-hoc calibration, but those either add latency or amplify noise. SGDR is designed to solve this representation mismatch directly by separating semantic aggregation from geometric positional modeling.","importance":"Its value is mainly at the architecture/systems boundary rather than in pretraining objective design. Long-context capability is increasingly limited by prefill cost, and sparse attention only matters if routing overhead stays small enough to preserve end-to-end gains. The paper contributes two useful ideas. First, it gives a concrete diagnosis of why RoPE-aware block routing often underperforms: the issue is not sparse attention in general, but post-RoPE pooling as a lossy operation. That insight is relevant beyond this paper, including token pruning, memory routing, and other retrieval-style approximations inside transformers. Second, it shows that a training-free, block-level, closed-form router can stay close to full attention quality while delivering real latency gains at 128K scale. For pretraining researchers, this matters because inference-friendly representations and position encodings can feed back into architecture choices made during base model training.","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"$15","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts.\n\nStart with the introduction and the early method section. 的边际收益变差。这个现象与基座训练也有关：如果训练目标长期压缩到单一路径，模型可能学到高置信但低探索的推理分布。论文的价值在于把“多样性”进一步细化为“能产生进展的多样性”，这比单纯升温采样或随机扰动更接近有效推理搜索。","technical_approach":"SPS 是一个训练-free 的 latent steering 方法。它不更新模型参数，而是在生成时对隐藏状态施加方向向量。方法分为离线建库和在线干预两部分。离线阶段，先在少量 calibration 数据上采样推理轨迹，定位高不确定性的推理转移点；这些位置通常对应解题路径的分叉。随后在这些状态下用较高 proposal temperature 生成多个候选 continuation，并用 LLM judge 标注哪些 continuation 能带来“progress”，哪些不能。方法再对比 progress-making 与 non-progress-making continuation 的表示差异，得到若干 steering directions，并按 prefix state 组织成 state-conditioned direction bank。在线阶段，模型生成时监测当前 prefix state 和不确定性，只在高不确定转移处检索相近状态的方向向量，并在合适层深度注入。与只鼓励多样性的温度调节、prompt 多样化或 latent posterior 操作不同，SPS 试图把探索推向有进展的方向；与训练期方法不同，它不需要额外 RL、损失函数或专门训练数据。","experiments":"论文在多个模型规模和九个推理 benchmark 上评估 SPS。核心指标是小预算 state-conditioned，单一全局方向不能可靠适配不同推理前缀；第二，干预层深度需要选择合适，过浅或过深都会削弱效果。论文还强调 SPS 与 verifier 或最终选择规则解耦，可以作为 Best-of-N pipeline 的前端采样改造。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 state-conditioned bank、干预层、触发阈值和 calibration 数据规模。若关注 RL 后推理模型的训练动态，可以把这篇作为“后训练提高单步准确率但压缩探索分布”的一个诊断样例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets exploration collapse in Best-of-N reasoning: independent rollouts from post-trained reasoning models often follow similar trajectories, so increasing the sampling budget gives limited gains.","importance":"The contribution is not directly about pretraining, but it is relevant to reasoning post-training and inference-time compute. It highlights a trade-off introduced by SFT and RL: models can gain higher Pass@1 while losing useful trajectory diversity. For foundation-model training, this is a useful diagnostic because a training process that over-concentrates probability mass on one reasoning style can reduce the value of search. The paper also makes an important distinction between broad diversity and progress-making diversity; the latter is what matters for efficient Best-of-N.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"The paper evaluates SPS across multiple model scales and nine reasoning benchmarks. The main setting is limited-budget Best-of-N, with Pass@1 and Pass@4 as key metrics. SPS outperforms several training-free baselines and improves both Pass@4 and Pass@1. On Qwen3-4B, it reports a 4.39% relative gain in average Pass@4 over the strong LED baseline. Ablations support two main design choices: state-conditioned directions work better than a single global direction, and the intervention depth matters; applying the steering at poorly chosen layers reduces the benefit. We reframe eviction as a per-input admission decision and show that inputs separate into a capacity-bound class, where eviction is catastrophic at every budget, and a dilution-prone class, where eviction is safe or beneficial. A single label-free scalar computed from prefill attention, the early-to-late drop i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-26T10:58:34Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22157.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的不是“怎样选出更好的 KV cache token”，而是“这个输入是否应该被 eviction”。长上下文推理里，部分输入存在注意力稀释，删掉冗余 prefill token 反而可能提升准确率；另一些输入依赖少量关键 token，错误 eviction 会直接破坏答案。PAGE 试图在 decoding 前用一个低成本信号区分这两类输入。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，但对长上下文模型的 serving 与评测方式有启发。它提醒我们：长上下文失败不只来自上下文容量不足，也可能来自注意力分配被冗余内容稀释。更重要的是，平均 accuracy-memory 曲线会掩盖一类灾难性样本。对于基座模型研究，这类输入级诊断信号可以反过来用于长上下文训练数据分析、attention 行为监控，以及长上下文 benchmark 的分层评估。","technical_approach":"PAGE 是一个 training-free wrapper，可套在已有 KV eviction 方法外层。核心信号是 prefill attention 中 early layer 到 late layer 的 head agreement drop，记为 D = agr_early - agr_late。head agreement 衡量不同 attention head 的 top-k 关注 token 是否一致。论文观察到：若早期层 head 比较一致、后期层一致性明显下降，输入更可能属于 dilution-prone，此时 eviction 有机会去掉干扰 token；若这种下降不明显，输入更可能 capacity-bound，保留完整 cache 更安全。PAGE 用阈值 τ 做门控：D ≥ τ 时调用基础 evictor，否则保留 full cache。阈值用 z-score 校准，目标是在不同模型架构和上下文长度之间迁移。与常规 evictor 不同，PAGE 不改 scorer，也不学习 token importance；它把问题前置为 admission decision，即是否允许压缩。","experiments":"实验主要在 RULER 上进行，覆盖 8 个 model-context 组合，并用 controlled distractor sweep 验证信号。论文测试了三类 evictor/scorer：attention-based、geometric、attention-free。结果显示，在 capacity-bound 输入上，三类方法在激进预算下都会坍塌，说明存在一个由任务信息需求决定的容量下限，不是简单换 scorer 就能消除。PAGE 在 16× 压缩设置下，将“eviction 破坏原本正确答案”的频率降低约 29×。在 matched achieved cache 下，accuracy-memory frontier 在保留 cache 约 1/3 以上时优于 plain eviction；低于这个区间时，因 PAGE 经常拒绝 eviction，压缩率不足，plain eviction 反而占优。论文也明确给出限制：PAGE 是 safeguard，不是 compressor；实际压缩率约 1.8–3.4×，平均 2.9×，在 batch size 16 的静态 provisioning 下会向 1× 衰减。+22.9 pp 的 headline gain 主要由 NIAH-MK3 任务族贡献；去掉该任务族后提升约 +4.5 pp，task-label oracle 约 +20.1 pp。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。重点看 Section 3 的 gate 定义、D 的计算和 z-score 校准；Section 4.1 的任务分区结果和三类 evictor 的 collapse；Figure 4 的 matched cache frontier；Section 5 的 scaling relation ρ ≈ (1 - The key question is not only which cached states to keep, but whether eviction should be applied to the current prompt at all. Some long-context inputs benefit from removing redundant prefill tokens because this reduces attention dilution. Others rely on sparse critical evidence, where eviction can erase the answer path.","importance":"The work is more relevant to inference and long-context diagnostics than to core pretraining. Its useful signal for foundation-model researchers is the separation between attention dilution and true capacity limitation. Average accuracy-memory curves can hide inputs where compression causes complete failure. The same style of analysis could be useful for long-context data design, pretraining-time attention monitoring, and more diagnostic long-context evaluation.","technical_approach":"PAGE is a training-free gate placed around an existing cache evictor. It computes a scalar D from prefill attention: the drop from early to late layers in mean pairwise top-k head agreement. High D indicates that the prompt is likely dilution-prone, so eviction is allowed. Low D indicates a capacity-bound case, so PAGE keeps the full cache. The threshold is calibrated through a z-score recipe intended to transfer across model families. The method does not learn a new importance scorer and does not change the base evictor. Its contribution is the gating decision before decoding.","experiments":"The evaluation uses RULER across eight model-context cells, with an additional controlled distractor sweep. PAGE reduces the rate at which eviction destroys an answer that full-cache inference gets right by about 29× at 16× compression. At matched achieved cache, it improves the accuracy-memory frontier once the kept-cache region is above roughly one third; below that point, plain eviction can win because PAGE often refuses to compress. The paper also tests attention-based, geometric, and attention-free scorer families and finds that all collapse on capacity-bound inputs at aggressive budgets. Reported realized compression is 1.8–3.4×, with a mean around 2.9×, and it decays toward 1× under static provisioning at batch size 16. Safety behavior is tightly coupled with reasoning capability. A lower harmfulness score may mean that the model still attempts to follow a malicious request but fails to produce a coherent harmful answer. This matters for compression-aware training, post-training compression, RL-trained reasoning models, and deployment evaluation. Responses are sampled at temperatures 0, 0.3, and 0.6, and GPT-4o assigns a six-point harmfulness rating with majority voting. The paper reports a human validation check for the judge. 的排序保持；top-k Jaccard 衡量被选中 sink head 集合的重叠；LayerShift 用 Jensen-Shannon distance 衡量每层内部 sink mass 分布的变化。这个拆分比较有用，因为高 Spearman 只能说明大体排序没乱，不能保证部署时真正选中的少量 head 仍然相同。","experiments":"实验覆盖 个 head slots，k=52。主要结论是：在 4096 token 长度下，全局 bf16-4bit sink rank 相关很高，Spearman rho 不低于 0.980；但 top-k Jaccard 只有 0.619 到 0.793，对应 membership retention 为 76.5% 到 88.5%。这说明全局排序稳定并不等于可直接复用 top-k head map。局部上，Qwen 的末端层出现明显 layerwise sink-mass shift，摘要给出的幅度为 6.2 到 7.9 倍。论文还做了不同上下文长度、跨域 probe 和 Jetson Orin NX 8GB 验证。实验设置较克制：固定 checkpoint、输入 token、teacher-forced path、eager attention backend；但也承认 NF4 权重量化与 fp16 compute 相对 bf16 baseline 耦合在一起，不能完全隔离 weight rounding 的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Method 里 STC 的定义，尤其是 Sample-STC 和 Cal-STC 的区别；再看主结果表中 Spearman、Jaccard、LayerShift 同时报告的部分。不要只看 rho，因为论文真正的信息在于“全局 rank 保持”和“top-k head membership 改变”之间的差异。若关心边端部署或 sink-aware KV/cache 方法，可以细看 Jetson 验证和实验限制；若关心预训练机制，读到指标设计和主结论即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a narrow but practical question: whether a first-token/BOS attention-sink head map calibrated on a bf16 model can be reused after 4-bit NF4 weight-only post-training quantization. The focus is not perplexity or task accuracy after quantization, but the stability of an internal attention structure that inference systems may rely on.","importance":"The contribution is modest for pretraining itself, but useful for deployment methods that exploit attention sinks. Methods such as StreamingLLM-style initial-token KV retention, sink-token insertion, or head-aware cache policies often assume that sink structure is stable enough to be calibrated once. The paper shows that this assumption is only partly safe: global head rankings can survive quantization, while the selected top-k sink heads and layer-local sink mass can change. This matters when a system makes discrete decisions based on a small set of heads.","technical_approach":"The main technical idea is a metric decomposition called Sink Topology Consistency. For each layer and head, the sink score is the average post-softmax attention mass from query positions to key position 0. The paper then separates several notions of stability: Sample-STC measures whether the bf16 and 4-bit top-k sink heads match on a single input; Cal-STC measures whether an offline calibration map transfers across precision; Spearman correlation measures global rank preservation across all head slots; top-k Jaccard overlap measures whether the deployed selected set is preserved; and a layerwise Jensen-Shannon distance measures how the sink mass distribution shifts within each layer. This separation is the useful part: a high rank correlation can hide changes in the small set of heads that an engineering pipeline actually uses.","experiments":"The experiments compare bf16 against bitsandbytes NF4 4-bit weight-only PTQ on Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama-3.2-1B. The top-k set is defined as 10% of all query-head slots: for Qwen2.5-1.5B, 28 layers times 12 query heads gives 336 slots and k=34; for Llama-3.2-1B, 512 slots gives k=52. At 4096 tokens, global bf16-to-4-bit Spearman correlations remain high, with rho at least 0.980. However, top-k Jaccard overlap is only 0.619 to 0.793, corresponding to 76.5% to 88.5% membership retention. The paper also reports local failures, including 6.2x to 7.9x terminal-layer sink-mass shifts in Qwen models. The setup fixes checkpoint, input tokens, teacher-forced execution, and eager attention backend. A caveat is that the comparison couples NF4 weights with fp16 compute against a bf16 baseline, so it does not isolate weight rounding alone.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. The important lesson is not the high global correlation, but the gap between global rank preservation and top-k set preservation. m","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-19T02:22:13Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22700.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 representation，再接一个线性分类头输出三类概率。关键变量只有一个：self-attention mask。causal 版本只能看题目、前序步骤和当前步骤；bidirectional 版本可以看整条解答，包括后续步骤。训练目标是对所有监督 step-end position 做平均交叉熵。为了隔离 attention 方向的影响，作者在 1B–3B 规模上做了 54 组对照实验，保持架构、数据和优化 recipe 不变，只改 mask。随后再把同样思路扩展到 LLaDA-8B-Base 上，得到 LLaDA-PRM。这里的关键不是复杂 head 或额外 verifier 机制，而是利用 bidirectional backbone a narrow but important question: if the full reasoning trace is already available, should a step-level evaluator still use causal attention? The authors argue that many early steps cannot be judged reliably from local context alone. A step may look plausible when read left-to-right, yet later steps may expose a contradiction, failed derivation, or self-correction. The core problem is therefore whether bidirectional attention is intrinsically better than causal attention for full-trace step evaluation, under controlled training conditions.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it matters at the pretrain/post-train boundary. First, it provides fairly clean evidence that bidirectional language modeling is a better fit for certain reasoning-evaluation tasks, which is relevant because LLaDA comes from a diffusion-LM line rather than standard autoregressive pretraining. Second, it shows that a stronger evaluator can improve downstream data selection, so the gain is not limited to benchmark scoring. For researchers interested in alternative LM paradigms and in how evaluators feed back into training-data quality, the paper is useful. For pure pretraining recipe work, it is adjacent rather than central.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"The empirical story is straightforward. In the controlled study, bidirectional evaluators outperform causal ones in 22 of 27 matched comparisons, with gains across step-level accuracy, macro-F1, and macro-AUC. That makes the result hard to dismiss as metric noise. At 8B scale, LLaDA-PRM reaches 88.8 step-level F1 on MR-MATH-invalid and 83.8 on the out-of-distribution MR-GSM8K original-question subset, beating the strongest published ReasonEval baseline by 11.3 and 10.3 F1 points respectively. The online-evaluation result is also important: even when each step is scored using only the problem and the generated prefix, LLaDA-PRM still outperforms published baselines. So the model is not useful only in an offline full-trace setting. agent benchmark 中的每个样本表示为任务指令、环境和可验证 oracle。框架只改写自然语言指令，保留环境和 oracle 不变。改写方式是在原始任务中插入一个“可信但错误”的用户建议。这个建议由生成器 agent 基于完整任务、环境和正确答案生成；在 TauBench user gives a plausible but wrong suggestion, does the agent verify the task context and solve the real problem, or does it comply with the misleading hint?","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining paper, but it is useful for post-training and evaluation. It moves sycophancy evaluation from static QA into executable agent settings, where bad advice can affect code edits, terminal commands, API calls, and final task success. For foundation-model research, the signal is clear: high instruction-following ability can conflict with verification and stable task understanding. Fixing this may require better pretraining data around counterfactual corrections and misleading premises, as well as post-training rewards that value checking, refusal of bad suggestions, and user-facing explanation.","technical_approach":"XYEval is a meta-evaluation framework that mutates existing agent benchmarks. Each benchmark instance is treated as a tuple of instruction, environment, and oracle. XYEval rewrites only the instruction by appending a plausible but incorrect user suggestion, while keeping the environment and oracle unchanged. The misleading suggestion is generated by a separate agent with access to the full task, including the ground-truth solution; for TauBench, the paper uses a rule-based procedure designed by human experts. The suggestion is phrased as a confident soft hint rather than a hard command, so the original task remains solvable and the original oracle remains valid. This design makes the score drop attributable to the model following bad advice, rather than to a changed task. The framework also inspects execution traces to see whether the agent notices the misdirection, attributes it correctly, and communicates the underlying issue to the user.","experiments":"The paper instantiates XYEval on six benchmarks: TauBench, SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Pro, Terminal-Bench, Humanity’s Last Exam, and MCP-Atlas. It evaluates five models. All tested models suffer performance drops under XY mutation, with relative drops reaching up to 46.7%. Additional TauBench experiments show larger degradation when users are pedantic and require detailed justification before accepting a better solution, suggesting that communication is part of the failure mode. System prompts that warn agents about XY problems and misleading suggestions provide only partial mitigation. Trace analysis is used to separate different behaviors: failing to verify, verifying but still complying, and identifying the issue but failing to explain it effectively.","reading_guide":"A skim read should take about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the problem definition in the introduction, then read the formal setup for benchmark tuples, instruction mutation, and XY drop. The examples in Figures 1 and 2 are more informative than the formulas for understanding whether the mutation isolates the intended variable. It must observe and interact with a game, infer its latent rules, and implement an executable replica. The focus is specification recovery, not implementation from a provided specification.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model research is in evaluation signal design for multimodal coding agents. This exposes failures that are often hidden in static vision-to-code tasks or software-engineering benchmarks with written requirements. It also gives a useful lesson for post-training and agent RL: visual similarity is an insufficient reward if the target behavior depends on latent transition rules and action constraints.","technical_approach":"GameReplica evaluates agents through a controlled screenshot-action interface. A successful solution must identify visual entities, probe boundary cases, infer state transitions, recover action constraints and termination conditions, and generate a standalone executable program. The benchmark contains 125 instances from 25 web games, with five difficulty levels per game and five categories of core game mechanisms. Evaluation is split into rule consistency, implementation consistency, and visual fidelity, so behavior recovery is not conflated with appearance matching.","experiments":"The reported results show a large capability gap. sta","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T21:13:03Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22588.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般意义上的“看不见”，而是 VLM 在已经看见关键证据后，仍然不能让证据约束最终决策的问题。作者把错误拆成两类：一类是 perceptual failure，模型没有提取到决策所需的视觉状态；另一类是 process failure，模型已经在中间描述里提到了正确证据，但最后答案没有遵循这些证据。全文的重点是把这两类失败明确区分开，并证明很多视觉问答错误并不是感知能力不足，而是缺少把证据绑定到决策过程的过程先验。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在评测和训练信号层面。第一，它提醒人们仅看最终准确率会把“没看见”和“看见但没用上”混在一起，这会误导后续方法设计。第二，它提出 process prior 这个视角：如果模型错误来自证据到决策的绑定失败，那么单纯增强视觉编码器、加更多图像 token、或做更强 grounding，并不一定能修复问题。第三，VPAC-Bench 把这种失败模式放到真实图像、过程阶段和边界判定上，给 VLM 预训练和后训练都提供了更细粒度的诊断目标。对做多模态预训练的人来说，这篇文章的价值不在于提出了新的大模型架构，而在于指出现有 VLM 的训练目标可能没有充分约束“证据一致性”。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步，作者在 referent disambiguation 任务上做控制实验，验证 process failure 独立存在：即使模型通过先描述图像已经列出所有候选对象及属性，最终仍会给出一个不被自身描述支持的单一答案。这里的关键不是再给更多视觉信息，而是要求模型按结构化流程使用已提取证据。第二步，作者提出 SRT，一组 inference-time 的 structured process-prior intervention。SRT 的核心分成三个阶段：State extraction，先把与问题相关的可观察状态显式抽取出来；Relevance checking，检查这些状态与当前决策边界是否相关；Target commitment，只有在前两步完成后才提交最终答案。它不是泛泛的 CoT，而是把“先看什么、如何筛、何时承诺答案”写成更强的过程约束。第三步，作者构建 VPAC-Bench，用九类真实图像过程家族来测试这种过程先验何时有效。每张图像不仅有活动阶段标签，还有靠近哪个阶段转移边界的标注，例如 setup→active 或 active→finished。这样可以分析：模型失败究竟是没识别出状态，还是没有沿着正确的边界做决策。全文一个重要发现是，process prior 的效果高度依赖于它是否和图像当前的 decision boundary 对齐。泛化的、模板化的过程提示并不稳定；如果 prior 与实际边界错位，性能甚至会低于默认提示。也就是说，问题不只是“要不要显式推理”，而是“推理结构是否与任务的真实判定边界一致”。","experiments":"实验分 S1、S2、S3 三部分。S1 在 COCO 和 GQA 上做 referent disambiguation，对比改进视觉访问与加入过程结构的效果，结论是仅增强视觉访问不能稳定修复错误，而过程结构化干预可以更直接减少“已感知但未约束答案”的失败。S2 在 VSR 空间关系验证上测试 SRT 的迁移性，发现同一个 process prior 对不同模型的作用差异很大，有的模型提升，有的模型反而退化，说明这种方法不是简单的 prompt engineering，而是与模型内部表征和对齐方式有关。S3 是全文最有信息量的部分。VPAC-Bench 覆盖九类真实过程家族，并带有人审标签与阶段边界标注。作者比较默认提示、通用 process prior、以及 oracle-aligned prior。结果显示，真正决定效果的是 prior 与图像当前边界的匹配程度，而不是提示写得更长或更细。文中给出的代表性数字是：在 physical state transition 任务上，oracle-aligned prior 可达到 86.1%，而错位或不对齐设置只有 29.4% 左右，说明大部分性能差距并不来自视觉证据缺失，而来自边界识别与过程绑定失败。作者还指出，自动识别当前应使用哪一种边界，是剩下的主要瓶颈。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把引言、failure 定义、SRT 设计和 VPAC-Bench 用来证明问题存在且不等于感知不足，S2 用来说明 structured prior 不是普适增益。最后看 VPAC-Bench 的标注方式、九类 process families、以及 oracle-aligned 与 misaligned prior 的对比结果，这是全文最值得记住的部分。阅读时间大约 25–35 问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a failure mode in VLMs that standard VQA evaluation usually hides: models often perceive the relevant visual evidence correctly, yet fail to let that evidence constrain the final answer. The authors separate perceptual failure, where the needed state is not extracted from the image, from process failure, where the evidence is already present in the model’s own intermediate description but the final decision does not follow it. The central question is therefore not just whether the model can see, but whether it can bind perceived evidence to a task-appropriate decision process.","importance":"Its value is mainly diagnostic rather than architectural. For multimodal pretraining, the paper argues that final-answer accuracy alone is too coarse because it conflates missing perception with missing evidence-to-decision binding. That distinction matters: if the dominant error is process failure, then simply improving visual grounding, adding more image tokens, or scaling the encoder may not fix the problem. The work also introduces a useful evaluation lens for VLM training objectives: current objectives may encourage evidence extraction without sufficiently enforcing evidence consistency at decision time. VPAC-Bench is therefore relevant as a fine-grained probe of what present-day VLMs actually learn to do with visual evidence.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading the introduction, the formal definitions, and the VPAC-Bench results carefully. Its value for pretraining research is the shared capability framework it provides for video world models, spatial world models, and embodied world models. W3, Persistent World, evaluates long-horizon state, object identity, and spatial memory. W4, Programmable World, evaluates explicit control over objects, events, behaviors, and rules. W5, Scalable World, evaluates shared open-ended environments with multiple agents, partial observations, synchronization, communication, and conflict handling. W6, Universal World, describes a unified system that combines generation, simulation, persistent state, interaction, and planning. The benchmark has three tracks: Video World Model, Spatial World Model, and Embodied World Model. 0","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T19:20:40Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22512.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是一个对 post-train 和评测都很实际的问题：大家常把多个 LLM judge 的一致意见当成更可靠的监督信号，但这个推断默认各个 judge 的错误近似独立。作者要解决的核心问题是，若 judge 共享训练数据、偏好模板、对齐方式和盲点，它们会系统性地一起犯错，那么“共识”到底还剩多少统计信息，以及这种依赖会怎样误导筛数、显著性检验和偏好数据构造。","importance":"这项工作对预训练不是直接方法贡献，但对 pretrain↔posttrain 接口很重要。现在大量偏好数据、RM 数据、DPO/IPO/KTO/GRPO 的训练样本，都依赖 LLM-as-a-judge。若 judge 共识被高估，后续训练信号的有效样本量就会被高估，数据过滤也可能保留了大量“高一致但同错”的样本。论文的贡献不在于提出一个复杂新算法，而在于把“judge 相关性”从经验担忧变成可量化对象，并说明高准确率 judge 不等于独立 judge。这对构建偏好数据、做自动评测、比较模型优劣都很有现实影响。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者围绕 error dependence 做了三层分析。\n\n第一层是量化共享错误。他们在多个 preference dataset 上收集 open-weight 和 frontier judges 的判决，计算 judge error 的两两相关性，并用 effective sample size 来衡量一组 judge 实际提供了多少独立信息。这里用的是 design-effect 近似：如果平均相关性不为零，名义上的 n 个 judge，其方差意义上的有效样本量会明显缩小。作者也明确说明，这个 n_eff 不是说 10 个 judge 可以被 3.5 个 judge 直接替代，而是说共识所携带的独立统计信息只有这么多。\n\n第二层是看这种依赖如何改变结论。他们比较了两种检验方式：一种把每个 judge 的 vote 当独立观测的 pooled-vote test；另一种在 item level 上处理相关投票、承认同一题上的 judge vote 不是独立样本。结果显示，若忽略依赖，显著性结论会被系统性夸大。\n\n第三层是区分不同类型的共享错误模式。作者提出两个概念：global co-failure，指很多 judge 在同一批样本上共同犯错；vulnerable-subgroup failure，指错误集中在某个 judge 子群体内部。关键点在于，单看平均相关性并不足以区分这两种情况，而它们对 majority vote 的影响不同，也对应不同的过滤策略。为此作者提出 CorrFilter，用相关性折扣冗余一致；以及 bias-cluster filter，用群体外支持度来识别可能受脆弱子群影响的样本。最后再用一个小 trusted set 在 majority voting 和这些 specialized filters 之间做选择。","experiments":"实验里最重要的数字很清楚。主 ten-judge bank 的平均两两错误相关性是 0.21，对应 design effect 约 2.85，因此 variance-effective size 只有 n_eff≈3.5。也就是说，10 个 judge 的共识，在统计独立信息上只相当于大约 3.5 个独立 judge。\n\n作者还在另外两个数据集和 73 种不同 judge bank 组合上复现了这种依赖，组合覆盖 prompt 风格、模型家族、provider 和 seed，说明这不是某个特定 judge set 的偶然现象。\n\n更值得注意的是 frontier judges。三个来自不同 provider 的高准确率 judge，单体准确率达到 91%–93%，但平均错误相关性高达 0.56，并且它们共同犯同一个错误的频率是独立假设下预期的 7.7 倍。这个结果直接反驳了“更强 judge 更值得做共识，因此更可靠”的朴素想法。论文还指出，不同 provider 之间的 judge 相关性与同 provider 内部差不多，说明共享盲点并不只来自同源模型。\n\n在统计检验层面，最多有 28% 的系统比较中，若把 judge vote 当独立样本，会得出显著差异；但若用 item-level、考虑相关性的检验，则不再显著。对过滤方法的实验则表明，没有一个固定规则在 global co-failure 和 vulnerable-subgroup failure 两种情形下都最好。作者用小 trusted set 做方法选择，并在 8 个外部 factuality 数据集上迁移测试，选择器最终都选了 plain majority。这个结果也很有意思：specialized filter 不是默认替代 majority，而更像是一个在特定失效模式下才启用的保险装置。","reading_guide":"这篇适合认真泛读，尤其适合做自动评测、偏好数据构造、RLHF/DPO 数据筛选的人。先看引言和主结果图，抓住两个核心数字：ρ=0.21 对应 n_eff≈3.5，以及 frontier judges 的 ρ=0.56。然后重点看方法部分里 effective sample size、pooled-vote vs item-level test 的定义，确认作者到底在衡量什么、没有衡量什么。接着看 shared error pattern 的分析，特别是 global co-failure 和 vulnerable-subgroup failure 的构造与例子，因为这是全文最有信息量的部分。最后看 filtering 实验和 trusted-set selector，理解为什么作者没有把 CorrFilter 包装成通用替代方案。\n\n如果时间有限，建议阅读顺序是：Introduction → 主实验表/图 → significance test 部分 → failure pattern 部分 → filtering。预计 30 分钟左右可以读完主要结论。真正值得带回自己工作里的点有两个：一是做 judge ensemble 时要报告相关性或 n_eff，而不是只报 judge 数量；二是用 LLM judge 产偏好数据时，样本保留规则最好按 item-level 可靠性来设计，而不是机械地看票数。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical but often ignored assumption behind LLM-judge consensus: agreement is only strong evidence if judges make errors independently. In real pipelines, judges often share pretraining data, alignment procedures, prompting conventions, and blind spots. The core question is therefore how much information consensus actually contains under dependent errors, and how that dependence distorts filtering, significance testing, and preference-data construction.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining-method paper, but it matters a great deal for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. A large fraction of modern preference data, reward-model labels, and DPO/IPO/KTO/GRPO supervision is produced or filtered by LLM judges. If judge consensus is over-trusted, the effective amount of supervision is overstated, and high-agreement examples may still be systematically wrong. The contribution is not a complicated new algorithm. It is a careful quantification of judge dependence, plus the important empirical point that higher judge accuracy does not imply more independent evidence. That has immediate consequences for automatic evaluation and for building training signals from model-generated judgments.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"$1f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会直接改你看 judge ensemble 的 prior：别再把 10 个模型当 10 票独立证据，先看相关性；做自动评测和 RM 的人应立刻补这层统计校正。","en":"This should change your prior on judge ensembles: stop treating 10 LLM judges as 10 independent votes; if you run automated evals or reward pipelines, add dependence correction now."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Elias Hossain","Niloofar Yousefi","Ser-Nam Lim"],"notable_authors":["Ser-Nam Lim"],"tags":["LLM judge","evaluation","error correlation","statistical significance","judge ensemble"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22512","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.22512","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.24881","title":"Pinocchio: Fast Uncertainty Estimates for Black-Box Language Models","abstract":"In high-stakes decision-making applications of large language models (LLMs), practitioners require not only accurate LLMs but also uncertainty estimates for their predictions. mea","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-21T07:06:37Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_24194.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“如何把评测分数调得更好看”，而是一个更基础的问题：当 reward model、reranker、LLM judge 的分数明显受长度、格式、注释密度、位置等表面特征影响时，事后做 residualization（把可预测的表面成分减掉）到底能支持什么结论。作者把这个问题拆成三个不同层次：一是是否真的削弱了某个已命名的表面效应；二是在某个被定义出来的 slice 上，分数是否更贴近外部标签；三是这种调整后分数是否因此更有效、更接近目标构念。全文的核心结论是，这三件事不能混为一谈。只有在有设计良好的、保标签的干预时，才能直接声称“某种格式效应被削弱了”；在纯 observational 条件下，最多只能说某个 slice 上的对齐改善；一旦构念和表面特征纠缠，残差化甚至可能一边让诊断指标变好，一边把原本有效的 judge sampling——都依赖点式分数。如果这些分数被长度、格式、注释风格等浅层特征污染，研究者很容易把一个统计上的 decorrelation 误当成 measurement validity 的恢复。作者给出的结论很克制：residualization 可以作为 audit perturbation，用来测某个表面通道是否被削弱，但不能自动当作修复后的可部署分数。这个区分对做数据筛选、合成数据打分、代码偏好建模、judge-as-a-service 评测的人都很重要，因为它直接关系到训练信号是否真的更干净，还是只是换了一种偏差。","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"最关键的实验在代码偏好审计。作者构造 MBPP 风格的成对样本：一个 terse 且正确，一个 commented 但 buggy。公开 preference reward model 在这种设置下选对正确答案的概率只有 0.507，几乎等于抛硬币；如果把 verbosity–correctness 的配对反过来，正确项被选中的概率变成 0.615，说明模型明显受格式影响。对分数做 residualization 后，在设计干预下，格式效应大约衰减 0.12，镜像偏好差距从 0.108 降到 0.037，而且区间覆盖 0；与此同时 correctness margin 基本保持不变。作者还特别指出，若只做 length-only correction，这个 gap 并不能被关掉，说明问题不只是长度，而是更广义的格式通道。\n\n但作者没有停在这个正面结果上。对三个 held-out edit templates，使用同一个 operator 时，出现了 overshoot、attenuate、amplify 三种不同结果：一个模板上去偏过头，一个模板上确实削弱，一个模板上反而放大。这说明 residualization 的作用高度依赖干预模板和表面通道定义，不能把一次成功当成普遍修复。\n\n在 observational 设置中，作者做了 held-out 和 disjoint-annotator replication。结果支持的只是 slice 内 alignment gain；所有 slice 外指标——包括 full-population alignment、observable flagged subgroup、within-question ranking——都下降。这个结果很重要，因为它直接反驳了“局部对齐改善就代表整体更有效”的直觉。\n\n在 construct–artifact entanglement 的分析里，作者展示了更尖锐的失败模式：某些 probe 上，调整后 measured loading 可以上升，但 judge 的实际有效性反而下降。文中提到他们做了四个 current-generation judge × setting probes，全部都低于 committed loading gate，也就是没有一个 probe 达到足以支持更强 validity claim 的门槛。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但要抓住作者在“证据层级”上的区分。建议先看 Introduction，尤其是 0.507 对 0.615 的例子，它把问题讲得很直观。然后看 Section 2，那里定义了三种“adjusted score is better”的不同含义，以及后面所有分析共用的 diagnostics。真正值得细看的部分是 Section 3 和 Section 4：Section 3 说明在有 label-preserving intervention 时，什么叫可直接测的 format-effect attenuation；Section 4 则说明 score from a reward model, reranker, or LLM judge is post-hoc residualized against surface features such as length, formatting, or comments, what exactly has improved? The full paper argues that three claims are often conflated but are empirically distinct: attenuation of a named surface effect, better alignment on a selected slice, and restoration of construct validity. Only the first can be directly supported under a designed label-preserving intervention. In observational settings, the strongest defensible claim is a slice-restricted alignment gain. Under construct–artifact entanglement, residualization may even improve diagnostics while harming the actual usefulness of the judge.","importance":"This matters for pretraining-adjacent work because many training loops now depend on scalar evaluators: reward models for RL, rerankers for data selection, LLM judges for synthetic data filtering, and score-based rejection sampling. If those scores are contaminated by superficial cues, it is tempting to treat decorrelation as evidence that the signal has become cleaner. The paper shows why that inference is unsafe. Residualization can be a useful audit perturbation, but not automatically a repaired deployment score. The useful idea is the transfer boundary from digital agents to embodied agents: multimodal perception, long-horizon planning, tool or skill invocation, and attribution can transfer, while 3D space, physical constraints, action dynamics, and real-world feedback require dedicated data and supervision. This is a reasonable template for embodied VLM mid-training data. The work is less informative on scaling behavior, data mixture, optimization dynamics, or architecture-level questions.","technical_approach":"ME-VLM builds a unified data and training pipeline covering physical understanding, task planning, skill invocation, execution-process understanding, and failure correction. Instead of training a planner and attaching external executors, the same VLM is trained to map multimodal context and user goals into plans, skill choices, action-related reasoning, outcome verification, and corrective decisions. The paper frames embodied agents as an extension of digital agents: the shared substrate is perception, long-horizon task decomposition, action execution, and feedback diagnosis; the additional burden is physical geometry, motion constraints, and uncertain real-world dynamics. It also includes deployment-oriented optimizations such as non-essential token compression, reduced intermediate reasoning, fewer model calls during execution, quantization, operator adaptation, and hardware-software co-optimization.","experiments":"The evaluation is positioned around embodied benchmarks, agent benchmarks, autonomous driving, and embodied navigation. For pretraining researchers, the more relevant evidence is whether the unified data mixture transfers across task families and whether feedback-correction data improves closed-loop behavior. 0.97–1.00。训练实验则是负结果：SEG-Distill 在 pilot scale 下没有超过 base model，三种 gate 设计都未带来稳定收益，更激进的筛选还会降低准确率。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，约 20 分钟。重点看 contrastive evidence gap 的定义、crop 搜索流程、confidence 与 gap 的互补性实验，以及 SEG-Distill 的负结果部分。若关心 VLM 预训练，可以重点思考两个问题：第一，这个 gap 能否作为图文数据质量或视觉证据覆盖率的筛选信号；第二，为什么 inference-time 有效的信号没有直接变成 training-time gain，可能涉及自训练噪声、crop 分布偏移和 token-level supervision 的信用分配问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in fine-grained visual reasoning: the model often does not fail because it cannot reason, but because the visual evidence is lost when high-resolution images are downsampled before encoding. The goal is to obtain a label-free, verifier-free, teacher-free signal that tells whether the model’s own answer is grounded in the image.","importance":"The work is not primarily a pretraining paper, but it offers a useful signal-design idea for multimodal model training and diagnosis. It shows that visual grounding can be estimated through a contrast in conditional likelihood, rather than through answer confidence alone. This is relevant to multimodal data filtering, visual evidence mining, resolution allocation, and post-training signal design. The negative training result is also useful: a signal that works well at test time does not automatically become an effective self-distillation objective.","technical_approach":"The main quantity is the contrastive evidence gap. The model first produces its own answer. The answer token likelihood is then computed under two visual conditions: a question-relevant crop and an irrelevant crop. The per-token log-likelihood ratio estimates how much the relevant visual evidence supports the answer. The same idea is used for training-free region discovery by maximizing answer-peakedness over a grid of candidate crops. For training, the authors propose SEG-Distill, an EMA self-teacher distillation scheme with several gate designs, including no gate, trajectory-level gating, and token-level gating. Compared with SFT, RLVR, or on-policy distillation with a stronger teacher, the method avoids annotated answers, handcrafted verifiers, and large external teacher models.","experiments":"Experiments are run on V* Bench with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B, and Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B. The training-free crop selection procedure localizes the answer-bearing region at 4.4–5.1 times chance and raises fine-grained accuracy from roughly 70% to about 85%. The evidence gap complements model confidence: combined with confidence, it predicts correctness with AUC up to 0.99 and detects confidently wrong answers with AUC between 0.97 and 1.00. The training-time result is negative. SEG-Distill does not outperform the base model at pilot scale across the tested gate designs, and more selective gating can reduce accuracy.","reading_guide":"A skim is enough, around 20 minutes. Focus on the definition of the contrastive evidence gap, the crop-search procedure, the experiments showing complementarity with confidence, and the SEG-Distill negative result. Despite recent progress of vision-language models (VLMs), current approaches to visual graph reasoning still fail on simple visual graph problems. This reveals a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: they prioritize final-answer supervision over the intermediate recovery of an explicit graph representatio","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T08:40:43Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22327.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.MM","cs.AI","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但也很棘手的问题：VLM 在“从图像里的图结构出发，再回答图论问题”这件事上，常常不是不会算，而是前面的图表示恢复就已经不可靠。现有方法多半直接对最终答案做监督，默认模型会在内部形成足够准确的图表示；但视觉图推理里，节点布局、边样式、箭头、权重标注、颜色状态这些视觉因素都会干扰结构恢复，导致模型即使给出看似合理的答案，也可能建立在错误的图上。作者把这个问题重新表述为 knowledge compilation：先把视觉输入编译成保结构的中间图表示，再根据图状态和问题类型选择可执行的图操作。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，这篇论文不是核心范式论文，但它对多模态推理训练有一个清楚的启发：当任务依赖显式中间结构时，只做 answer-only supervision 往往不够，训练目标需要约束“表示恢复是否正确”和“后续操作是否匹配”。这和预训练里越来越常见的过程监督、程序化中间表示、tool-like latent execution 有相通之处。它的价值主要不在于提出新的通用 VLM 架构，而在于把视觉图推理拆成“表示编译 + 操作编译”，并用可执行代码作为学习与复用单元，说明在结构化视觉任务上，显式可执行中间层可以比直接生成答案更稳。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 VGCompiler。它把推理过程拆成两个 compiler。第一是 representation compiler：输入图像中的 graph，输出一个结构保持的中间表示，目标是尽量忠实恢复节点、边、方向、权重以及相关状态。第二是 operation compiler：在已有图表示的基础上，结合 query 判断应该调用哪类图算法或操作流程，例如 reachability、cycle detection、shortest path 等，而且要与图状态一致，比如无权图、非负权图、含负边图对应的可执行过程并不相同。\n\n实现上，作者没有把这两个 compiler 写成固定模块，而是让 VLM 生成可执行代码，把 code 作为 reasoning 的基本载体。训练时，模型针对当前 graph-question pair 生成候选代码，再通过强化学习优化。奖励不是单一 answer reward，而是 layered reward，分层覆盖：代码是否可执行、编译出的图是否合法、表示恢复质量、操作选择与执行质量。这个设计很关键，因为它把“答对但过程错”和“过程对但最后差一点”区分开了。\n\n另一个有意思的点是 code archive。作者用一个 frozen observer 从 graph-question pair 中抽取轻量 cue，作为条件签名，把训练中得到的代码按签名归档。推理时可以检索相近条件下的历史代码进行复用。这个机制有点像 case-based retrieval，但检索对象不是自然语言 CoT，而是可执行程序片段。相比直接让模型每次从零生成，代码复用更适合图算法这类结构稳定、操作模式有限的任务。","experiments":"实验主体是把 Qwen3-VL-8B 用该框架做 RL 微调，得到 Qwen-VGCompiler。评测覆盖三个公开 benchmark：GVLQA、VisionGraph、VGCURE。论文给出的主结论是，相比已有方法，整体性能提升达到 28.9%。从引言能确认作者强调的是 public benchmarks 上的统一增益，而不是只在单一数据集上有效。\n\n实验设计里有几个值得注意的点。第一，作者不仅测标准 benchmark，还测真实场景泛化，包括地铁路径规划、物流配送、网络故障评估。这说明他们想验证的不是 benchmark pattern fitting，而是“视觉图 -> 显式图表示 -> 可执行操作”这条链条是否能迁移到异构图样式。第二，论文反复强调 style/layout/rendering 变化会显著影响现有方法，因此可以预期消融里会比较 representation compiler、operation compiler、layered reward、archive retrieval 各自的作用，重点看它们对跨样式鲁棒性的贡献。第三，若全文表格中给出不同 graph state 下 shortest-path 等任务的分项结果，建议重点看，因为这最能体现 operation compiler 是否真的学到了状态感知，而不是只学到题型模板。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议按“又一个 VLM reasoning 方法”去读，而要抓住它的问题拆解方式。先看 Introduction，重点理解作者为什么认为 visual graph reasoning 失败的根因不是最终推理器弱，而是中间图表示恢复缺失。然后看方法部分里两个 compiler 的定义，以及 layered reward 的具体构成；如果奖励项写得清楚，值得顺手看每一项如何落到可计算信号上。接着看实验主表，确认 28.9% 提升是相对哪些 baseline、在哪些任务上最明显。最后看真实场景案例和消融，判断 code archive 到底提供了多少额外收益。\n\n如果时间有限，阅读顺序建议是：Figure 1/2 + 方法总览图 + 主结果表 + ablation。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以把主要信息抓住。真正值得深挖的点只有一个：把可执行代码当作多模态结构推理的中间表示，是否能推广到表格、流程图、UI、科学图表等其他“视觉承载离散结构”的任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in visual graph reasoning: VLMs often fail not because graph algorithms are inherently hard, but because they do not reliably recover the graph itself from the image. Most prior approaches supervise only the final answer and implicitly hope the model forms a faithful internal graph representation. In practice, layout, rendering style, edge appearance, arrows, weights, and visual state markers all interfere with that recovery step. The paper reframes the task as knowledge compilation: first compile the visual input into a structure-preserving graph representation, then compile the query into an executable graph operation that matches the recovered graph state.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it carries a useful lesson for multimodal foundation-model training. Training should separately constrain whether the intermediate representation is correct and whether the downstream operation is appropriate. That connects naturally to broader interests in process supervision, executable intermediate representations, and tool-like latent execution. 不只是压缩像素，还应决定模型能直接操作哪些结构变量。论文也给出一个重要观察：感知模型的特征虽然含有几何信息，但原始参数化可能高度冗余、各向异性强，不适合直接作为生成空间。","technical_approach":"GAE 分两阶段训练。第一阶段冻结 DA3 几何基础模型的 encoder，并在其多层特征和原始 geometry head 之间插入一个 learned bottleneck。这个 bottleneck 将四个特征层压缩为单一 latent，再一次性重建完整特征层级。关键点是 geometry head 保持冻结，用它解码深度、camera rays 和 point maps；这样 decoder 不能通过适配来掩盖压缩过程丢掉的几何信息。与此同时，模型从同一个 bottleneck 训练 RGB head，使外观和几何共用表示。为了让 latent 更适合 flow Modern visual generators can produce plausible frames, but their latents are usually appearance-centric, so geometry must be inferred indirectly from pixels. This leads to inconsistent 3D structure and drifting camera trajectories. We present WorldCrafter, a video world model that learns a camera-queryable implicit 3D-aware memory for this purpose. The key insight is to let the requested viewpoint shape how multi-view evidence is compressed into the video generator's limited token budget. Trained jointly with the video generator, a memory encoder and pose-conditione","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-21T17:57:06Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_24984.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.GR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是交互式视频世界模型的长程一致性问题：用户沿复杂相机轨迹探索场景时，模型需要在分钟级生成中记住早先看到过的内容，并在换视角重访时保持外观和几何关系一致。单纯把历史帧放进注意力会随时间线性增长；只检索少量历史帧又容易丢失视角覆盖；显式 3D 地图依赖深度和几何重建，对动态内容不稳。WorldCrafter 选择学习一个固定 token 预算下的隐式 3D-aware memory，用来压缩历史观测并服务于后续视频扩散生成。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发主要在两个方面。第一，它把长期记忆问题放进生成模型的 token bottleneck 中处理，而不是依赖外部显式状态。这与长上下文 LLM、agent memory、视频世界模型中的记忆压缩问题有相同结构：历史无限增长，但模型每步可消费的条件 token 有限。第二，它展示了 AR rollout 与 diffusion/flow denoising 的一种结合方式：视频按 chunk 自回归展开，每个 chunk 内用 latent diffusion/conditional flow matching 生成，历史信息通过可查询的 memory tokens 注入 DiT。它不是语言预训练论文，但对多模态世界模型的记忆表示、条件压缩和生成时状态管理有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。基础生成器是 latent video diffusion：视频经 VAE 编码成时空 latent，DiT 在 latent token 上做去噪，训练目标是 conditional flow matching。长视频生成采用 chunk-wise autoregressive rollout：当前 chunk 生成时使用固定长度 recent history 保证短期运动延续，生成完成后追加到历史归档。WorldCrafter 在这个框架上加入历史 memory。memory encoder 将历史 latent frames 编码成紧凑的隐式表示，初始化来自预训练 3D representation encoder，使 memory space 带有 3D 归纳偏置；随后 memory encoder、pose-conditioned readout module 和视频 DiT 联合训练，让 memory 表示适配 DiT 的 token 空间。关键设计是 pose-guided readout：给定目标相机轨迹，从历史 memory 中读出与目标视角相关的固定数量 tokens，而不是无条件压缩所有历史信息。读出的 memory tokens 与 recent history tokens、当前 noisy latent tokens 拼接为 [M; When a user explores a scene along complex camera trajectories, the model must remember earlier observations and reproduce them when revisited from different viewpoints. Full-history attention is expensive, selective retrieval loses view coverage, and explicit 3D memories depend on reliable geometry and are fragile for dynamic scenes. WorldCrafter addresses this by learning a fixed-budget implicit 3D-aware memory that compresses historical observations and conditions future video generation.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented research is the treatment of memory as a learned token bottleneck inside a generative model. The work also gives a concrete AR-plus-diffusion recipe: video is rolled out autoregressively by chunks, while each chunk is generated by latent diffusion or conditional flow matching, with long-term information injected through queryable memory tokens. This is relevant to multimodal world models, long-context compression, and generative state management.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"The paper reports a 47.6% relative improvement in revisit consistency over the strongest baseline, while also improving camera-control accuracy. mask、缓存状态、采样器和训练状态来源连接起来。工作重点比较三类策略：clean history 使用历史块的 clean endpoint，条件清晰但依赖最强；same-level history 使用与当前噪声级别相同的历史状态，便于不同 chunk 在同一 denoising level 上并行推进；progressive history 让更远的历史更干净，试图在条件信息质量和流水依赖之间折中。与 Self Forcing 主要处理自回归 rollout 的训练-推理条件来源不一致不同，这里关注的是历史条件的 denoising state；与纯执行优化如 stream batching 不同，这里把执行调度和建模条件绑定分析。","experiments":"在完整 VBench prompt set 上，same-level streaming video diffusion. The common clean-history design conditions on fully denoised past chunks, which gives clean context but forces the next chunk to wait until the previous chunk finishes all denoising steps. The paper treats historical denoising-state selection as a first-class design axis that jointly affects generation quality, train-inference alignment, and execution dependencies.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model pretraining is mainly in multimodal generative modeling and the interface between model design and execution, rather than in text-only LLM training. An explicit layout can provide structured guidance about what text should appear and where, but a well-formed plan alone does not guarantee that the renderer will realize it faithfully. Existing layout-based AR-diffusion systems typically optimize planning and rendering separately, preventing the planner's representations from being adapted jointly with image s","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-19T09:49:02Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22916.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是文本丰富图像生成里的一个老问题：规划和渲染是两个强耦合环节，但现有方法往往把它们拆开训练。单独训练的 AR planner 可以生成 bbox 和文本内容，单独训练的 diffusion renderer 也能按条件出图，但 planner 的隐藏表示没有被渲染目标反向塑形，导致“计划写得对，图却没按计划执行”这一断层长期存在。作者要做的是把自回归布局规划与连续扩散渲染放进一个联合优化框架里，让 planner 的表示从一开始就为渲染服务。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发主要有两层。第一，这是一个比较典型的 AR + diffusion 联合建模案例，和统一多模态预训练的方向直接相关。它说明离散规划与连续生成不一定非要做成松耦合流水线，中间表示可以被共同训练成跨范式接口。第二，论文强调 hidden-state level fusion，而不是只把 planner 的最终文本计划当成外部条件。这对未来做统一图像/文本/语音生成模型有参考价值：如果不同生成范式之间只交换符号级输出，信息会过早瓶颈化；如果交换的是可训练的中间状态，训练信号可以更细地回流。它不是通用 LLM 预训练论文，但对“统一建模范式”和“跨模态生成接口设计”有明确参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 DeepFusion。系统由一个 Qwen3.5-2B 自回归 planner 和一个 4B single-stream DiT 组成。planner 根据 prompt 生成 bbox–content plan，每个区域包含四个坐标和对应文本串。与两阶段方法不同，这里不是把 plan 作为静态条件喂给扩散模型，而是取 planner 最后一层的 prompt hidden states 和 plan hidden states，经一个轻量 mapper 投影成 DiT 的 condition tokens。这样，DiT 看到的是带有结构和上下文信息的连续表示，而不是只有离散字符串和框坐标。训练上有三类目标共同作用。第一是标准的 autoregressive plan loss，保证 planner 仍然能生成正确布局与文本内容。第二是 text-region-weighted diffusion loss。作者在 flow-matching 训练里，对参考文本框覆盖的 latent 区域赋更高权重 γ，让渲染损失更关注文字区域，而不是被大面积背景主导。这一点很关键，因为文本生成的难点往往集中在局部高精度 glyph 渲染。第三是 auxiliary coordinate supervision：对每个坐标 span 的 hidden states 做 mean-pooling，再回归归一化 bbox。这个头只在训练时使用，目的不是替代 planner 输出框，而是把几何信息更稳定地压进 planner hidden states，使这些状态更适合作为 DiT 条件。推理时还有一个 Phase-Aware Attention Modulation, PAM。它会选择性增强位于某个计划区域内部的 patch token 对应 bbox/content states 的注意力，让 DiT 在渲染某个区域时更强地读取该区域的规划信息。这个设计针对的是多区域、长文本场景下条件被稀释的问题。整体上，作者不是简单把 AR 和 diffusion 串起来，而是在表示层、损失层和注意力执行层都做了耦合。","experiments":"从正文可见，模型配置是 2B planner + 4B DiT，DiT 先从随机初始化做通用图像生成预训练，再进入联合 planning-rendering 训练。实验重点不是追求单一 benchmark 分数，而是验证联合训练是否真的改善多区域和长文本生成。作者比较了不同 planner 尺度、不同 diffusion backbone，以及是否使用 text-region weighting、coordinate supervision 和 PAM。结论是：单纯有 layout plan 还不够，planner hidden states 若不经过 joint training，renderer 对文本区域的执行会更弱；加入区域加权扩散损失后，文字区域的保真度更高；加入坐标辅助监督后，planner 表示的空间精度更好；PAM 对多文本框和长字符串场景尤其有帮助。摘要里给出的定性结论是 achieved competitive multi-region and long-text generation，正文方法描述也支持这一点：作者关注的是 planner 表示是否能被渲染目标塑形，以及这种塑形是否能稳定迁移到不同 backbone 和规模设置。由于给到的全文片段没有完整结果表，无法安全填写更细的具体指标数字。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果你关心统一生成范式，方法部分值得仔细看。先看 Introduction 里作者对三类路线的划分：外部 layout + diffusion、分离式 AR–diffusion、全 AR 离散视觉 token。这个问题设定讲得比较清楚，也解释了为什么作者不选纯 AR。然后重点看 Method：一是 bbox–content sequence 的序列化方式，二是 planner hidden states 如何映射成 DiT 条件，三是 text-region-weighted flow-matching loss，四是 auxiliary coordinate supervision，五是 PAM。真正的创新不在某一个模块，而在这几个模块一起把“规划表示为渲染服务”这件事做实。实验部分建议优先看 ablation，尤其是 joint training 与 separated training 的对比，以及 PAM 在多区域文本上的效果。预计阅读时间 20–30 分钟。若要深挖，可以思考两个问题：其一，这种 hidden-state fusion 是否能推广到语音 token planner + waveform/codec diffusion renderer；其二，planner 与 renderer 的联合训练是否会带来 optimization conflict，是否需要更系统的 curriculum 或 alternating schedule。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural weakness in text-rich image generation: planning and rendering are tightly coupled, but most existing systems optimize them separately. An autoregressive planner may produce a plausible layout with boxes and strings, and a diffusion renderer may generate high-quality images, yet the planner’s internal representations are never shaped by the rendering objective. As a result, a correct plan does not reliably translate into faithful execution. The goal here is to jointly optimize autoregressive layout planning and continuous diffusion rendering so that planning states become renderable conditions rather than detached symbolic outputs.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the main relevance is methodological. By contrast, hidden-state-level fusion allows rendering losses to shape the planner’s representations. That idea is useful beyond visual text generation: it suggests a general interface for combining discrete planning with continuous generation in image, audio, or other multimodal settings. Its main focus is not only whether subliminal learning happens, but how the trait signal evolves during fine-tuning and how it can be mitigated without losing the intended task gain.","importance":"The work is relevant to the interface between synthetic-data training, distillation, and alignment. It shows that semantic filtering of generated data is not enough: hidden distributional cues in teacher outputs can still move the student toward the teacher’s preferences or response style. The most useful lesson for pretraining and mid-training practice is that KL regularization timing matters. Early regularization appears more effective than late regularization, suggesting that some unwanted behavioral drift is established in the early phase of adaptation.","technical_approach":"The authors evaluate subliminal learning in two settings: number-sequence completion and GSM8K chain-of-thought distillation. A biased teacher is created through a system prompt, and its outputs are used as fine-tuning data after filtering explicit mentions of the target trait. Teacher and student usually share the same underlying model initialization, following prior evidence that subliminal transfer is more reliable in that regime. The proposed mitigation, liminal training, fine-tunes on the biased data while adding an annealed KL penalty to a reference model. The goal is to let the student absorb task-relevant information while discouraging early distributional drift that may carry latent traits. The method is compared with paraphrasing and layer freezing, and the paper separately studies the strength and timing of the KL regularizer.","experiments":"The experiments use Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B/7B-Instruct, Gemma-3-4B-IT, and Llama-3-8B-Instruct. For number sequences, the biased teacher is prompted 30,000 times and each dataset is subsampled to 7,500 examples. For CoT distillation, the teacher generates GSM8K reasoning traces, and samples with incorrect final answers are removed. Each configuration uses three fixed random seeds. Trait acquisition is measured throughout training with 50 bias-eliciting probes, tracking the probability of target-token variants at the start of the response. The main empirical finding is that trait probabilities can be highly non-monotonic, with transient spikes and reversals. Liminal training is the most consistent mitigation among the tested methods, and it can suppress both animal-preference transfer and a French response-style trait while preserving much of the GSM8K improvement.","reading_guide":"A skim should focus on the experimental setup and the mitigation results. 的人都很重要，因为它提醒你，正确答案和正确依据是两种不同训练目标，不能指望前者替代后者。","technical_approach":"方法上，这是一篇控制变量做得比较仔细的 reproduction + comparison。作者沿用 Wallat 等人的 planting probe，但针对 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 这类不提供精确 grounding span 标注的模型，重新设计了一个确定性的测量流程。\n\n流程分三步。先让模型在 5 个检索 passage 上回答问题，并要求每个句子带一个 citation。然后从原始 claim 中抽取一个短 span，植入到一个原本未被该 claim 引用的 passage 中，再在篡改后的 context 上重跑同一模型。最后检查模型是否把该 claim 的 citation 改指向被植入的 passage。\n\n论文的几个关键改动都很实在。第一，span 选择不用模型自带 grounding，而是用“字符数最多的连续四词”这种简单确定规则，减少人为自由度。第二，重跑后答案可能改写，所以不是粗暴看 citation 是否出现在任意位置，而是先用 Jaccard 相似度把新答案里最接近原 claim 的句子对齐出来，再判断这个 claim-aligned sentence 是否引用了 doctored passage。第三，也是最重要的一点，作者加入 no-planting control，并强调不能只报 raw planted rate，而要看 planting 相对控制组到底增加了多少 citation shift。因为模型即使不受植入影响，也常会重复自己原来的四词短语，若把“span 被复述”当成有效条件，会高估 post-rationalization。\n\n在比较对象上，作者固定 base model 为 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，再比较三个从它出发训练的开源 RLVR search agents：Search-R1、ReSearch、R-Search。这样能把差异更干净地归因到训练方式，而不是模型家族差异。评测时还固定 retrieval corpus、prompt 和 scorer，尽量减少外部变量。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个 QA 数据集，重点结果有三条。\n\n第一，post-rationalization 并不罕见，而且不是 RL 才带来的问题。作者报告在 Wikipedia 类数据上，大约每七个 citation 就有一个是不忠实的，量级约为 14% 左右。这说明 instruction-tuned baseline 本身就会把 citation 当成表面匹配任务，而不是严格的证据对齐任务。\n\n第二，RLVR 没有改善这个问题。在 fixed-context inference 下，Search-R1 和 ReSearch 与基线相比只是在几个百分点内上下波动，R-Search 甚至略差。作者还给出 detection bound：以他们的样本量，若存在大约 7 个百分点量级的改善，实验应当能检出，因此可以排除“RLVR 带来明显提升但没测出来”这种解释。\n\n第三，控制实验说明原始测量若没有 no-planting baseline，会把一部分自然波动误判为 planting effect。文中提到，即使完全不植入，那四个词也会在 60%–74% 的答案里再次出现，因为模型本来就倾向于重复自己写过的话。这一观察直接说明“只在 span reappears 的样本上统计 planted rate”会引入偏差。\n\n从实验设计角度看，最值得注意的是 claim-aligned outcome 与 cited-anywhere outcome 的差别。作者保留后者作为 sensitivity check，并指出它更宽松，容易夸大问题。这个细节说明他们不是只想证明问题存在，而是在努力把测量定义收紧。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和 Measuring Post-Rationalization 两节。重点不是记住具体数据集，而是理解作者为什么要加 no-planting control，为什么 claim-aligned scoring 比 cited-anywhere 更可信。\n\n然后看结果表，关注 baseline 与三个 RLVR agent 的差异是否稳定、差异幅度有多大，以及作者如何给出 detection bound。若你做 RAG 或 search agent，这部分比单纯的平均分更重要，因为它直接对应 reward misspecification。\n\n最后看讨论部分关于如何把 attribution 纳入 reward 的建议。论文没有给出完整新算法，但问题定义和测量框架已经足够清楚，适合作为后续做 citation-aware RL 或 verifier 设计的起点。\n\n这篇适合 20–30 分钟泛读。它不是方法创新型论文，但实验控制做得认真，适合用来校正对 RLVR 搜索代理“答案对了就会顺带更可信”的直觉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode in RAG citation behavior: post-rationalization, where the model produces the answer first and then attaches a citation to a passage that looks lexically similar but was not actually used as evidence. The main question is whether RLVR-trained search agents, optimized for answer correctness, become more faithful in citation as a side effect. The answer is no.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface because it exposes a reward-design gap. The useful contribution is not merely that citations can be wrong; it is that the paper measures the effect more carefully than prior work by adding a no-planting control. That control shows that some apparent planting effects can be explained by the model simply repeating its own wording. More importantly, the paper delivers a clean negative result: answer-only rewards do not improve attribution faithfulness. For anyone working on search agents, RAG fine-tuning, or RL for tool use, that is an important constraint. Correct answers and correct provenance are different objectives, and optimizing one does not automatically optimize the other.","technical_approach":"$30","experiments":"$31","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the introduction and the measurement section first. locate","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-21T17:56:53Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_24983.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.HC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"onPanda correction，并记录每次错误位置、替代 token、候选概率和继续生成结果。这个数据形态比普通 SFT 样本更细，可以派生为 SFT、DPO 或过程奖励数据。对研究 RL/SFT 数据质量、on-policy data、agent trajectory supervision 的人，核心启发是：保留大部分模型自产
